reports/esmc_articulated_root_cause_2026-09-19.{md,png,json} (Chinese report).
HPC3 CPU632468 COMPLETE0:0 in54s (4CPU16GB/noGPU, actual37.58s) covered128
initial/selected × two-seed saved-coordinate cases. GPU632473 COMPLETE0:0 in1:27
covered selected100 TRAIN35/166 with seed12345, actual four weighted losses and
combined parameter gradients. No optimizer update, retraining, held-out access or
full-length rerun. No live jobs/observers for this audit; do not repeat it.

Independent QA PASSED: NumPy projection max3.20e-14 A, scalar1.37e-12 A²,
three-factor VJP additivity4.92e-14 relative, independent central FD max5.48e-8,
GPU/CPU coordinate gradient max1.81e-6; BF16 parameter sum mismatch0.434%/0.371%.
Exact saved raw/adapted and train/eval replays; weights/features/adapters unchanged;
actual1trunk/1structure/0confidence. All75CPU/71GPU source files and128 coordinate
NPZ gradient artifacts, parameter Gram matrices, logs/accounting/hashes verified.
Two focused tests include factor additivity, translation identity, structural
zeros, Gram conflict and positive-scale invariance/Jacobian scaling; lint passes.

Confirmed computational mechanism: positive per-residue radial scale about CA is
invisible to output/loss but rescales orientation/internal Jacobians by1/s while
nondegenerate. All60,944 actual nonzero residue/loss radial derivative checks
maxrelative3.12e-13. Final median-of-case median rawCA-C0.546 A, N-plane0.552 A;
smallest perpendicular scale0.006855 A (above1e-4, so zero fallbacks is not proof
of well-conditioned derivatives). Same-output retracted-coordinate counterfactual
changes weighted raw gradient norms by median ratios A3.092,F3.785,R3.155,D3.959;
output error<=7.24e-14 A. Retraction changes raw lengths AND shapes: do not attribute
all3–4x to uniform scale alone. Adding another differentiable projection to the
existing graph leaves the composite chain rule unchanged; not a demonstrated fix.

Final64 factor norms (A/.1F/R/10D): translation .523/.161/~0/.575;
orientation4.21/4.76/~0/3.19; internal3.38/.344/2.60/~0. R cannot train rigid pose;
10D's fixed intra-residue distances plus C–N cannot train internal angles. About
16.31% raw atoms are structurally unused by probes; their zero direct gradients
are expected.88 unobserved raw atom entries have gradients through probes, while
unobserved final-output label gradients are zero; no missing GT was introduced.

Final64 negative-total directions: output harms no component; raw harmsF1/R4,
retracted harmsnone. Initial raw harmsR32/64. Actual global parameter directions
lower all4 losses in both audited cases; no general loss-conflict/ESMC-disconnection
claim. Combined ESMCprojection norms73.673/60.863, Pairformer58.838/58.444,
atomdecoder371.035/294.379. Component A/F/R/D norms35:229.45/109.14/288.61/66.90;
166:156.81/122.98/91.11/97.69. Clip10 scales.02364/.02907 do not establish effective
AdamW learning-rate reductions; no optimizer-history step was simulated. Shared
parameter subgroups can have local conflicts despite globally descending direction.

These facts establish parameterization sensitivity and supervision coverage, not
proof that removing scale freedom alone resolves the failed capacity gate. Next
research action: design one bounded scale-freedom intervention or explicit latent
pose/internal parameterization, verify unchanged expressivity/GT-free inputs and
stable derivatives, then predeclare one controlled comparison if justified. Do
not launch a loss grid, extend632192 or simply compose the projection twice.
No new architecture/training protocol has yet been selected. Original full
ESMC/all-heavy-atom/confidence/calibration/held-out/full-cost objective stays open.

## Decisions
- Use PDB/mmCIF experimental coordinates as GT; UniProt/SIFTS are provenance and traceability only.
- Keep the universal GT schema multi-chain-aware, but use a monomer-first training view: exactly one generated protein chain instance, no nucleic-acid or other-polymer chain instances, primary X-ray/EM/neutron method, one coordinate model, length 20--1024.
- Preserve missing residues/atoms as masks; do not silently impute them. Keep modified residues and mask unmapped modified side chains.
- Store exact 100% sequence identity grouping only. Preserve near-identical constructs and small mutations; keep each exact group in one split.
- Authoritative SI implementation is Biopython `Bio.Align.PairwiseAligner`; exact SHA256 is only a lossless candidate index.
- Main sequence conditioner is frozen ESMC-600M final residue layer, with pinned model/code revisions and BF16 sharded safetensors. ESMC-300M is an ablation; ESM3 structure-track outputs are excluded.
- Official Protenix Mini-ESM remains a compatibility baseline, not the active ESMC model.
- Do not tune on `frozen_temporal_test`; use `temporal_dev_v1` for all method selection. Keep the 15-group strict low-homology set frozen.
- Do not claim thermodynamic truth, sequence-pretraining cleanliness, or novel early stopping. Report the declared PDB-label cutoff and ESMC contamination limitation.
- Teacher-pair jobs must run each shard in a private working directory because Protenix writes relative `./esm_embeddings`. Any logged per-target `Data error` must make the Slurm task fail.
- Run DiamondHill teacher shards one per MI250 GCD (`ROCR_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0..7`), in two waves. Keep their output independent from the hpc2 mirror.
- Per explicit user instruction, do not cancel hpc2 c4 array `12771049` until all 16 DiamondHill shards finish and the full QA marker/result proves complete. Any supervisor or QA failure preserves the hpc2 jobs.
- The user explicitly permits installing Gemmi because it has no dependency burden. hpc3 `fold` currently has Gemmi 0.7.5; its import, CIF parsing, repo tests, and Protenix smoke are validated.
- Accept the mixed hpc2/hpc3 c2 corpus for paired teacher analysis because every artifact was revalidated uniformly on DiamondHill. Preserve source/job provenance per shard; this acceptance establishes artifact integrity, not numerical equivalence between GPU backends.
- Freeze the Stage 1B teacher split at 14,400/1,600 exact groups. The loader must verify sequence and c2/c4 atom-key identity, retain explicit missing-atom masks, require the expected recycle counts, and never impute prediction coordinates.
- The first refiner target is c4 C-alpha rigidly Kabsch-aligned into the c2 C-alpha frame. This removes arbitrary global rigid transforms without changing internal geometry. Treat the 32-target overfit as an implementation gate only.
- Per explicit user instruction, run all subsequent OneStepFold training on hpc3. DiamondHill is the accepted source/archive for the teacher corpus, not the training host. Reuse the existing hpc3 ESMC-600M cache rather than recomputing features.

- On hpc3, activate with `module load anaconda3; conda activate fold`. For Protenix use `LAYERNORM_TYPE=torch`, torch triangle kernels, and `PROTENIX_ROOT_DIR=/data/user/shuang886/Folding/runtime_cache/protenix_v1_1`.
- Use Slurm arrays as independent one-GPU jobs. Do not request an eight-GPU whole-node allocation for independent inference points.
- `i64m1tga800ue` is the preferred A800 partition; teacher jobs request 8 CPUs, 64G, one A800, and 8h. Adjust only after observing actual runtime.
- The local repo is the source of code/docs/reports. Push material changes to `origin/main`.
- Preserve the legacy `src/fastglycan` package; active protein code is under `src/onestepfold`.

## User Preferences
- Parallelize independent experiments when they answer a necessary question and save time; avoid redundant computation and unmotivated training grids. Reuse spare allocated CPU resources for independent assessment where possible.
- Verify HPC state and output files directly over SSH; Slurm `COMPLETED` is not sufficient without artifact QA.
- Prefer parallel independent jobs and deterministic, resumable artifacts.
- Keep research claims narrower than diagnostic evidence; record material decisions in `.agents/memory/decision_log.md`.
- Use concise, evidence-backed updates and report blockers plainly.
- Always respond to the user in Chinese.

## Important Files
- `docs/hpc2.md`, `docs/hpc3.md`: current partition, resource, and launch guidance. The old `docs/hpc.md` was moved by the user.
- `docs/gt_protocol_v1.md`, `schemas/gt_schema_v1.json`: frozen GT contract.
- `configs/monomer_view_v1.toml`, `configs/gt_quality_v1.toml`: monomer and quality policies.
- `docs/esmc_cache_v1.md`: ESMC cache contract and pinned revisions.
- `scripts/select_teacher_pairs.py`: deterministic teacher-group selection and standard-AA filtering.
- `scripts/slurm_teacher_pair.sh`: isolated teacher shard runner and error-to-nonzero enforcement.
- `scripts/submit_teacher_pair_array.sh`: two-setting Slurm array launcher.

## Commands
- Local tests: `pytest -q` (currently passes with one expected skip).
- Check repo: `git status --short`; latest fixes are `79da1e8`, `d967818`, and `f250626`.
- On hpc2, monitor: `module load slurm; squeue -u shuang886; sacct -X -j 12771048,12771049 --format=JobID,State,ExitCode,Elapsed,AllocCPUS,MaxRSS,NodeList`.
- Validate formal outputs after completion:
  `PYTHONPATH=/hpc2hdd/home/shuang886/Folding/catalog_pilot_code/src python /hpc2hdd/home/shuang886/Folding/catalog_pilot_code/scripts/validate_teacher_pairs.py --input-root /hpc2hdd/home/shuang886/Folding/stage1b/teacher_pairs_v3/input --output-root /hpc2hdd/home/shuang886/Folding/stage1b/teacher_pairs_v3/output_isolated --output-json /hpc2hdd/home/shuang886/Folding/stage1b/teacher_pairs_v3/validation.json`.
- A valid teacher corpus requires 16,000 c2 files, 16,000 c4 files, zero missing members, and no data-error logs.
- Build the frozen paired manifest with `scripts/build_teacher_pair_manifest.py`; validate the actual training-data contract with `scripts/validate_teacher_pair_manifest.py --workers 8` in an environment containing NumPy and Gemmi.
- DiamondHill control/status: `ssh pc@DiamondHill 'tail -n 50 /media/PM982/onestepfold/stage1b/teacher_pairs_v3/output_diamondhill/control/supervisor.log; tail -n 50 /media/PM982/onestepfold/stage1b/teacher_pairs_v3/output_diamondhill/control/full_qa_watcher.log'`.
- hpc2 Slurm is not on hpc3's default noninteractive PATH; query through hpc3 with `/opt/slurm/bin/squeue` or load the `slurm` module explicitly.
- hpc3 Slurm commands are in `/bin`; validated jobs are `628495` (CUDA/BF16) and `628527` (full Protenix smoke). The hpc3 GPU node exposed an NVIDIA H100 80GB and CUDA 12.8.

## Open Issues
- The old formal arrays `12768989/12768990` are invalid and must not be used: shared ESM temporary files caused partial, mismatched outputs. The first isolated retry also exposed unsupported amino-acid symbols and was cancelled.
- The Protenix runner can log `Data error` while exiting zero; always use wrapper exit status plus validator counts.
- Stage 1B data generation, QA and both coordinate/state learned pilots are complete. The current unresolved question is training the fresh ESMC core directly on observed experimental structures while preserving the frozen data contract.
- No full emulator is justified yet. Coordinate-only variants and controlled-pair comparison failed; the c1 pair-state MLP reduces state loss but worsens teacher-relative decoded geometry. The accepted 64-group experimental-GT comparison shows modest gains over c1, so teacher-relative failure must not be described as experimental harm. ESMC-600M core implementation and one-step interface are verified; trained quality, the formal 1,600-group gate, confidence and total inference cost remain unfinished.
- DiamondHill internal SSH/data transfer is healthy. Default routes and DNS work, and Anaconda returned HTTP 200; earlier `Errno 101`/PyPI SSL EOF events were intermittent outbound instability, so prefer the local wheel cache. Network is not the active compute bottleneck.

## Next Steps
1. Preserve the completed and independently verified 128/64 learned pair-state negative result. Do not restart jobs, extend epochs merely because state loss falls, or run full-data training.
2. Response training, full decoding, corrected exact QA, final archives and selected-checkpoint experimental-GT assessment are complete. Do not repeat them or extend the failed teacher-side configurations.
3. Capacity task631201, final QA/archives and geometry/noise diagnosis are complete. Do not rerun or extend it. Geometry-aware supervision and varied-RNG derivative audit631479 are accepted; scaled-loss training631506 and observer1691 are complete; the actual-gradient audit631551 is also complete, with next controlled0.1-weight experiment declared above. Preserve accepted data/masks; confidence calibration, formal held-out quality and end-to-end cost remain unfinished.
4. A future learned result must improve decoded all-atom and internal-distance metrics, match c2 quality with measured lower inference cost, and satisfy the formal held-out gate before scaling. Keep ESMC-600M and one structure evaluation as explicit unfinished original-objective requirements. Parallelize only independent necessary work; the baseline GT assessment has already used spare CPU without another GPU.

## Handoff Notes
- Do not restart dataset construction or ESMC extraction; those layers are accepted and frozen.
- DiamondHill c4 is complete and accepted by exhaustive artifact QA; do not restart or create a competing launcher. hpc2 c4 `12771049` has already been cancelled as agreed.
- Teacher computation, consolidation, and full paired validation are complete. The accepted DiamondHill corpus combines hpc2 c2 shards 0--11, hpc3 c2 shards 12--15, and DiamondHill c4 shards 0--15; do not restart inference unless the data contract changes deliberately.
- The exact-group split and loader are now accepted too. Start from `teacher_pair_manifest_v1`; do not create an alternative split or touch the frozen temporal test during refiner development.
- hpc3 is the sole training host for the next stage. Its accepted data marker must be present before launching a training job; do not fall back to DiamondHill training merely because it retains the source copy.
- The 32-target overfit checkpoint is diagnostic and must not be promoted as a trained model. Use it only to debug the established input/target/training path.
- The hpc3 bounded pilot checkpoints are also diagnostic: `pilot_512x256_v1` and `pilot_4096x1600_v1` both failed the predeclared validation-ratio gate and must not be presented as successful emulators.
- The geometry pilot `pilot_geometry_4096x1600_v1` is complete, revalidated, and also failed the joint gate. Resume from its accepted artifacts and comparison report; do not relaunch it or infer that improved CA error alone justifies full training.
- `engramfold/` is an independent shared project that also consumes protein data and Protenix checkpoints. Do not move or modify it or its shared assets while working on OneStepFold unless the user explicitly requests cross-project changes.
- The project has intentionally stopped treating MeanFlow and predictive routing as the default method. The evidence supports “skip expensive late recycles by predicting their refinement,” with a cheap global coordinate refiner as the first test.
